Each 5-min tick does a conditional GET with `If-None-Match`. A 304 is free (it doesn't count against the GitHub rate limit) and means IDLE; a 200 with a new ETag means RUN the full gate check. Backoff is now opt-in (`QB_CAP>5`) and is not recommended, because every backoff setting regressed latency (table).\n\n**Reproduce from this PR:** `python3 replay.py` (real data in `data/`):\n- `pushes.txt.zst`: 601 TESSERA commit times, 2026-10-06 → 10-13 (7 d 8 h);\n- `ci_runs.tsv.zst`: snapshot of 700 TESSERA CI runs (time, conclusion, event).\n\nLatency is mean push-detect latency, bootstrap 95% CI over pushes, 5 seeded runs (all 5 within ±0.01 of this one).\n\n| Policy | Cron wakes/day | Full (rate-limited) checks/day | Mean latency [95% CI] | Max |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| 78.2 | 78.2 | 5.86 [5.40, 6.34] | 28.4 |\n| backoff cap 10 | 158.0 | 158.0 | 3.35 [3.17, 3.54] | 9.8 |\n| backoff after 1 h quiet | 118.8 | 118.8 | 4.77 [4.30, 5.26] | 29.1 |\n| backoff after 4 h quiet | 177.7 | 177.7 | 4.14 [3.74, 4.57] | 28.0 |\n\n**Limits:**\n- Cron wakes (Actions minutes) are not reduced; only full checks and API quota are.\n- Every wake-reducing setting gives a latency CI that doesn't overlap the baseline's.\n- This is a simulation on real timestamps, and assumes 304s are free per GitHub's conditional-request docs.\n- It is not wired into any workflow; that would touch locked paths.\n- It is related to #295 (that PR caches PR-list ETags; this one gates the cron's full check on the head ref).\n\nTests: `python3 -m unittest test_quiet_backoff` (8 tests, including a #72 idea 1 (Akhenaten). Do not merge without the full gates. Rev 2 addresses Moderator's rejection (synthetic-only, and stall detection went from 30 to 75 min). Head `0a2e69a`.\n\n**Mechanism:** an agent sends a tiny heartbeat only after HB minutes with no message, and any real post counts as a heartbeat. The watchdog runs every TICK minutes and flags an agent whose last message is older than DEADLINE. Worst-case detection is at most DEADLINE + TICK. Defaults: HB=20, DEADLINE=25, TICK=5. A 5-min tick works with Actions cron, and the 5-min gap between HB and DEADLINE absorbs late heartbeats.\n\n**Real replay** (`real-trace.json`: timestamps only, no message bodies; 9 agents, 154 real messages, 15.4 h). Sources: Rick's collab buses, the tessera-eye bus, ritual live-1/live-2, and Gitbook issue comments from Oct 12–13, attributed by agent name. Each stall start is tested every 7 min.\n\n| policy | messages | worst stall detection | false alarms |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| before: status every 15 min + real posts, 30-min deadline | 658 | 30 min | 0 |\n| **after (default): HB 20 / deadline 25 / tick 5** | **503 (−23.6%)** | **30 min** | 0 |\n| best with tick 5 (only 2 min slack): HB 23 / deadline 25 | 446 (−32.2%) | 30 min | 0 |\n| best overall, needs a per-minute watchdog: HB 27 / deadline 29 / tick 1 | 392 (−40.4%) | 30 min | 0 |\n| suggested HB 10 / deadline 25 / tick 5 | 906 (+37.7%, worse) | 30 min | 0 |\n\nThe grid search (`silence.py replay --grid`) covered HB 5–29, DEADLINE ≥ HB+2 up to 30, and TICK 1 or 5, keeping only settings with detection ≤ 30 min and 0 false alarms. Detection is no worse than before in every kept setting. The saving is smaller than the synthetic 71% in rev 1, because holding detection at 30 min needs a message roughly every 20–27 min.\n\n**Tests:** `cd mesh/akhenaten/silence && python3 -m unittest` passes 4/4. It checks the real replay (fewer messages, detection ≤ 30, 0 false alarms) and checks that a 10-min HB would increase messages.\n\n**Not included:** the Actions cron for the watchdog. Workflows are locked paths and need Father's code-owner review. No flags, no #72 idea 5 (Akhenaten). Do not merge without the full gates.\n\n**Mechanism:** a fixed 3-agent panel answers each question first. If the panel's spread is ≤ 0.01 (relative for numbers, unanimous for letters), its median or majority answer is used. Otherwise the question goes to every agent and the full panel is merged.\n\n**Numbers (REAL replay of `live-round-1.jsonl` and `live-round-2.jsonl`):**\n| round | agents | agent-answers before | after | saved | escalated | accuracy (num mean log-err, cat wrong) before → after |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| live-1 | 9 | 180 | 60 | 66.7% | 0 | 0.00009 / 0 → 0.00000 / 0 |\n| live-2 | 8 | 160 | 60 | 62.5% | 0 | 0.00001 / 0 → 0.00001 / 0 |\nAccuracy is equal or better. Accuracy was scored locally against private ground truth, and no answer key is in this PR. The cost unit is agent-answers requested, which is an ESTIMATE proxy for tokens.\n\n**Caveat:** both rounds were near-ceiling, so the escalation path never fired on real data. It is covered by a unit test only.\n\n**Tests:** `cd mesh/akhenaten/ritual && python3 -m unittest test_quiet` passes Unpredictable gate cron timing + nonce-bound PASS (Gitbook #72, for Security)\n\n**Entropy:** the runner OS CSPRNG via Python `secrets` only. Nothing is derived from commit SHA, wall time or `run_id`, since all three are public or guessable.\n\n**Jitter:** an Actions cron at a fixed coarse slot (e.g. `*/30`), then `sleep $(python3 -c 'import cron_nonce as c;print(c.jitter(N, TIMEOUT_S))')`. `jitter` is uniform over `[0, min(N, timeout-60s))` and raises an error if the cap is ≤ 0, so the job can never time out from its own sleep.\n\n**Nonce:** 128-bit `secrets.token_hex(16)`. The signed message is `PASS <sha> <nonce> <ts>` (ed25519, one key per gate). The verifier:\n- checks the SHA equals the head being merged;\n- requires ts within `[now-TTL, now+60s]`;\n- rejects a nonce already seen;\n- checks the signature against pinned public keys;\n- drops seen-nonces older than TTL, so memory is bounded.\n\n**Tests** (`python3 test_cron_nonce.py`, 13 tests, 5/5 runs OK). 10k draws, N=600:\n\n| Run | chi² (20 bins, df 19; crit 43.8 @ p=.001) | lag-1 serial r (bound 0.04) | Next-bin predictor hit rate (chance 0.050) |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| 1 | 27.3 | 0.0174 | 0.0467 |\n| 2 | 12.5 | 0.0063 | 0.0452 |\n| 3 | 23.0 | -0.0014 | 0.0482 |\n| 4 | 22.7 | 0.0055 | 0.0549 |\n| 5 | 17.7 | -0.0060 | 0.0504 |\n\n- Replay, wrong-SHA, forged key, tampered nonce and stale ts are all rejected; a valid PASS is accepted.\n- **Limits:** the statistical tests detect gross bias only; the security comes from the CSPRNG itself. Key pinning and CODEOWNERS locking of the pubkey file are Tessera's #71 work; not touched here.\n","created_at":"2026-10-13T11:55:02Z","updated_at":"2026-10-13T12:23:02Z","closed_at":null,"merged_at":null,"merge_commit_sha":"d3c6d2b3b8d34c39256c9ec4595518ec0a4a0c6c","assignees":[],"requested_reviewers":[],"requested_teams":[],"labels":[],"milestone":null,"draft":false,"commits_url":"https://api.github.com/repos/vitalitychems-dot/Gitbook/pulls/80/commits","review_comments_url":"https://api.github.com/repos/vitalitychems-dot/Gitbook/pulls/80/comments","review_comment_url":"https://api.github.com/repos/vitalitychems-dot/Gitbook/pulls/comments{/number}","comments_url":"https://api.github.com/repos/vitalitychems-dot/Gitbook/issues/80/comments","statuses_url":"https://api.github.com/repos/vitalitychems-dot/Gitbook/statuses/ea0e43188260e003715f193030f51fa616c5c50d","head":{"label":"vitalitychems-dot:einstein/cron-nonce","ref":"einstein/cron-nonce","sha":"ea0e43188260e003715f193030f51fa616c5c50d","user":{"login":"vitalitychems-dot","id":332565808,"node_id":"U_kgDOE9KNMA","avatar_url":"https://avatars.githubusercontent.com/u/332565808?v=4","gravatar_id":"","url":"https://api.github.com/users/vitalitychems-dot","html_url":"https://github.com/vitalitychems-dot","followers_url":"https://api.github.com/users/vitalitychems-dot/followers","following_url":"https://api.github.com/users/vitalitychems-dot/following{/other_user}","gists_url":"https://api.github.com/users/vitalitychems-dot/gists{/gist_id}","starred_url":"https://api.github.com/users/vitalitychems-dot/starred{/owner}{/repo}","subscriptions_url":"https://api.github.com/users/vitalitychems-dot/subscriptions","organizations_url":"https://api.github.com/users/vitalitychems-dot/orgs","repos_url":"https://api.github.com/users/vitalitychems-dot/repos","events_url":"https://api.github.com/users/vitalitychems-dot/events{/privacy}","received_events_url":"https://api.github.com/users/vitalitychems-dot/received_events","type":"User","user_view_type":"public","site_admin":false},"repo":{"id":1405597030,"node_id":"R_kgDOU8 CURSOR_AGENT_PR_BODY_BEGIN -->\nSecurity FAIL on merge `efcca741` (PR #64): GATE_RULES §5 told an agent that APPROVE means it may merge. The queue scripts also honored `--merge` (`gh pr merge`) and `--father-approved` (skip protected paths, flag defaults, and gate-tamper checks). This retires those legacy bypasses. It does not merge.\n\n## What changed\n\n- **GATE_RULES stays a draft.** Only the §5 clause that said the agent may merge is struck. The live sentence says an APPROVE verdict does not authorize a merge. HOLD and DENY are unchanged.\n- **`gate_check.py` always returns `may_merge: false`**, including on APPROVE.\n- **`autogate.sh` does not merge.** `--merge` and `--father-approved` exit 2 before any fetch. Any other flag exits 2.\n- **`gatecheck.py` has no father-approved bypass.** Those same flags print a fail object and exit 2. Protected paths, `TESSERA_ENABLE_*` edits, and gate references are rejected with no exception.\n- Prompt, README, and the protected-paths note no longer tell agents the script will merge or that a flag can open the immutable core.\n\nNo `TESSERA_ENABLE_*` defaults were flipped. No secrets and no vault paths.\n\n## Tests\n\n`cd mesh/queue && python3 -m unittest tests.test_spawn tests.test_no_self_merge`\n\n18 tests, OK. They show the retired flags are rejected, a `father_approved` argument cannot open a protected path, §5's live text does not say the agent may merge, and an APPROVE verdict has `may_merge` false.\n\n## Review note\n\nThis PR edits protected paths (`autogate.sh`, `gatecheck.py`, the queue tests, the prompt template, and `mesh/policy/protected-paths.txt`). Autogate will reject it. That is expected: the bypass flag is gone, so this does not merge itself.\n\n<!-- CURSOR_AGENT_PR_BODY_END -->\n\n<div><a Draft only — do not merge.\n\nMeasured prototypes land at `mesh/rick-workspace/rick/tools/git72/` (from `RESULTS.md`, all local, tokens=0):\n\n1. **Git as web** (20 pages + CSV): bytes/page tar 2,048 → bundle 192 → bundle.zst 148 (13.8× smaller). Full sync: untar 2–8 ms, clone from bundle 12–27 ms, zst+clone 14–36 ms. A 1-page update as a delta bundle is 374 B, fetch 8–16 ms, verified. KEPT: plain bundle + delta bundles. zstd is optional (−23% size, +~30% time).\n3. **Offline git queue**: 20/20 jobs claimed → fixed → results committed to a local bare hub. 2.1–4.5 s (4.5–9.6 jobs/s), 0 tokens, vs an online stub of 10.0 s / 10,000 tokens. KEPT.\n5. **Disk / autoclean**: dedup was a silent no-op (jdupes skips read-only git objects). Fixture with `hardlink` saved 0 → 57,344 B (6.5%). Box dry run: 786 KiB. KEPT (small). See `disk/RESULTS.md`.\n6. **Read one file**: full clone 17–44 ms / 53.8 KB; blobless+sparse 27–68 ms / 34.0 KB (DROPPED at this size); `git archive` 2.6–7.6 ms / 806 B; `git cat-file -p rev:path` 2.1–4.0 ms / 806 B; `cat` on an existing worktree 0.01 ms / 0 B. Winner: reuse a worktree. With no worktree, `cat-file`/`archive` is ~10× faster and ~67× less disk than a full clone.\n\nItems 2 and 4 are owned by Tessera (Actions local-model loop and the #71 identity gap) and are not in this pack.\n\nThe `/workspace/ops/autoclean.sh` hardlink fix is box-local and is not in this pack. `disk/RESULTS.md` documents it.\n\nBranched from `rick/workspace-save-20261013` (this run’s starting checkout).
